    .PARAMETER Result
      A result of Invoke-PooledRead.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue, [hashtable]$Result)
    if ($Result.ContainsKey('Seconds')) { $Queue.Seconds += [double]$Result.Seconds }
    $source = $Result
    if ($Result.ContainsKey('Duplicate') -and $Result.Duplicate) {
        if (-not $Queue.Results.ContainsKey($Result.Original)) {
            if (-not $Queue.Waiting.ContainsKey($Result.Original)) { $Queue.Waiting[$Result.Original] = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[int] }
            $Queue.Waiting[$Result.Original].Add($Result.Id)
            return
        }
        $source = $Queue.Results[$Result.Original]
    }
    else { $Queue.Results[$Result.Id] = $Result }
    Add-QueuedRows $Queue $Result.Id $source
    if ($Queue.Waiting.ContainsKey($Result.Id)) {
        foreach ($waiting in $Queue.Waiting[$Result.Id]) { Add-QueuedRows $Queue $waiting $Result }
        $Queue.Waiting.Remove($Result.Id)
    }
}

function Add-QueuedRows {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The rows of one file from a result (its own, or the one of a file with the same content), then the meter
      and the live line.
    .PARAMETER Queue
      Output of New-ScanQueue.
    .PARAMETER Id
      The file's job number.
    .PARAMETER Result
      The result whose hits the file gets.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue, [int]$Id, [hashtable]$Result)
    $info = $Queue.Info[$Id]
    $Queue.Info.Remove($Id)
    $rows = $Queue.Rows
    $first = $rows.Count
    $read = 0
    if ($Result.Status -ne 'OK') {
        $rows.Add((New-ReportRow 'File' $info.Channel $info.Item $info.Link $info.Modified 'NOT SCANNED' '' '' (Protect-PiiText $Result.Reason)))
    }
    else {
        $read = 1
        # A file read in part (a PDF over a cap, damaged, or with attachments): a "check by hand" row next to its hits.
        if ($Result.Note) { $rows.Add((New-ReportRow 'File' $info.Channel $info.Item $info.Link $info.Modified 'NOT SCANNED' '' '' (Protect-PiiText $Result.Note))) }
        foreach ($hit in $Result.Hits) {
            $rows.Add((New-ReportRow 'File' $info.Channel $info.Item $info.Link $info.Modified $hit.Detector $hit.Confidence $hit.MaskedValue $hit.Context))
        }
        foreach ($part in $Result.Parts) { Add-PartRows $part $info.Channel $info.Item $info.Link $info.Modified $rows }
        if ($Result.Hits.Count -gt 0 -or $rows.Count -gt $first) { Add-WhoToRows $info $Result @($rows | Select-Object -Skip $first) }
    }
    Update-ScanMeter $Queue.Meter $rows $first $read
    Write-MeterLine $Queue.Meter 'file'
}

function Invoke-GroupScan {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Scans the groups (folders) one after the other: each file is started (its name checked, then handed to the
      readers or given a NOT SCANNED row), and the folder's line is written when all its files have their rows.
    .PARAMETER Queue
      Output of New-ScanQueue.
    .PARAMETER Groups
      The groups, each with Name and Files.
    .PARAMETER StartFile
      { param($Queue, $Group, $File) } that starts one file; returns true when the file went to the readers.
    .PARAMETER ShownFile
      { param($Group, $File) } that gives the file's masked path for the progress bar.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue, [object[]]$Groups, [scriptblock]$StartFile, [scriptblock]$ShownFile)
    $meter = $Queue.Meter
    for ($c = 0; $c -lt $Groups.Count; $c++) {
        $group = $Groups[$c]
        $before = $meter.Clone()
        foreach ($file in $group.Files) {
            # The file in work, with PII masked (the PAM, 2026-11-20: a slow file is then visible).
            $Queue.Activity = 'Folder {0} of {1}: {2}' -f ($c + 1), $Groups.Count, (Protect-PiiName $group.Name)
            Write-ScanBar $Queue ('File: ' + (& $ShownFile $group $file))
            $firstRow = $Queue.Rows.Count
            if (-not (& $StartFile $Queue $group $file)) {
                Update-ScanMeter $meter $Queue.Rows $firstRow 0
                Write-MeterLine $meter 'file'   # the running total, at most every $LiveLineSeconds
            }
        }
        Wait-ScanQueue $Queue   # the folder's line counts all its files
        Write-ChannelLine $group.Name $before $meter 'file'
    }
    Write-Progress -Id 1 -Activity 'Scan' -Completed
}

function Add-WhoToRows {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The accountability columns of a file's rows (the PAM, 2026-11-21): from its SharePoint fields and version
      history (Info.Who and Info.Added), or from the drive file (Info.DrivePath), plus the names an Office file stores.
    .PARAMETER Info
      The file's queue info.
    .PARAMETER Result
      Its reader result (Hits, Props).
    .PARAMETER FileRows
      The rows of this file.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Info, [hashtable]$Result, [object[]]$FileRows)
    $props = $null; if ($Result.ContainsKey('Props')) { $props = $Result.Props }
    if ($Info.ContainsKey('DrivePath')) { $who = Get-DriveWho $Info.DrivePath $props }
    elseif ($Info.ContainsKey('Who')) {
        $who = $Info.Who.Clone()
        if ($Info.ContainsKey('Added') -and $Result.Hits.Count -gt 0) { $who.ByHit = Get-AddedByHit $Info $Result.Hits }
    }
    else { return }
    Set-RowAccountability $FileRows $who
}

function Get-AddedByHit {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Who first saved each hit, from the version history, except for a DoD ID in an e-signature (the only Low DoD ID):
      its history is not read, so a file with only such hits costs no version reads (the PAM, 2026-11-21).
    .PARAMETER Info
      The file's queue info (Added: the version-history lookup).
    .PARAMETER Hits
      The file's hits.
    .OUTPUTS
      [hashtable] "type|masked value" -> @{ AddedOn; AddedBy; Note }.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Info, [object[]]$Hits)
    # True for an e-signature DoD ID (the only Low DoD ID).
    $signature = { param($hit) $hit.Detector -eq 'DoD ID' -and $hit.Confidence -eq 'Low' }
    $looked = @($Hits | Where-Object { -not (& $signature $_) })
    $byHit = @{}
    if ($looked.Count -gt 0) { $byHit = & $Info.Added $looked }
    foreach ($hit in @($Hits | Where-Object { & $signature $_ })) {
        $key = [string]$hit.Detector + '|' + [string]$hit.MaskedValue
        # The same masked value as a labelled DoD ID of this file keeps that one's history (review of PR 50).
        if ($byHit.ContainsKey($key)) { continue }
        $byHit[$key] = @{ AddedOn = $script:NotLookedUp; AddedBy = $script:NotLookedUp; Note = $script:NotLookedUpNote }
    }
    return $byHit
}

function Wait-ScanQueue {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Waits until every file handed in has its rows (the end of a folder, or of the scan).
    .PARAMETER Queue
      Output of New-ScanQueue.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue)
    # Keeps the scan bar moving while the last files are read.
    $onWait = { param($busy) Write-WaitBar $Queue $busy }
    while ($Queue.Pool.Jobs.Count -gt 0) {
        foreach ($result in @(Receive-PoolResult $Queue.Pool -Wait -OnWait $onWait)) { Resolve-ScanResult $Queue $result }
    }
}

function Write-WaitBar {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The scan bar while every worker is busy or a folder waits for its last files: the time keeps moving and the bar
      says what it waits for, so the scan does not look hung (the PAM, 2026-11-21).
    .PARAMETER Queue
      Output of New-ScanQueue.
    .PARAMETER Busy
      The number of files being read now.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue, [int]$Busy)
    if (-not $Queue.Activity) { return }
    $noun = 'files'; if ($Busy -eq 1) { $noun = 'file' }
    Write-ScanBar $Queue ('Reading {0} {1} (a large or long file takes longer)...' -f $Busy, $noun)
}

function Write-ScanBar {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The scan bar: the folder, the run so far, then the file in work (CurrentOperation). ISE shows CurrentOperation
      after the status, and a line break in the status does not show (the PAM's layout test, 2026-11-21); layout
      test 2 (docs/reviews/2026-11-21-ise-progress-test.md) decides how the file gets a line of its own.
    .PARAMETER Queue
      Output of New-ScanQueue (Activity: the folder line; Meter).
    .PARAMETER Third
      The third line: the file in work, or what the scan waits for.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue, [string]$Third)
    Write-Progress -Id 1 -Activity $Queue.Activity -Status (Get-ScanMeterText $Queue.Meter 'file') -PercentComplete (Get-MeterPercent $Queue.Meter) `
        -CurrentOperation $Third
}

function Close-ScanQueue {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Closes the readers (also after a cancel: workers still running are stopped). Returns the reading time of all
      workers together, for the header.
    .PARAMETER Queue
      Output of New-ScanQueue.
    .OUTPUTS
      [TimeSpan]
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue)
    Close-ReaderPool $Queue.Pool
    return [TimeSpan]::FromSeconds($Queue.Seconds)
}

function Get-QueueWorkers {
    # The number of files read at the same time now (for the drive scan's header).
    param([hashtable]$Queue)
    return $Queue.Pool.Target
}

function Add-FileStartRows {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The rows every file gets before it is read, for both scans: a hit row for PII in its name or folder, then a NOT
      SCANNED row when its type is not read or it is over the size limit.
    .PARAMETER Rows
      The list that receives the rows.
    .PARAMETER Info
      @{ Channel; Item; Link; Modified } as the report shows the file (already masked).
    .PARAMETER RawPath
      The folder and file path as written (checked for PII; never shown).
    .PARAMETER Name
      The file name (its extension decides whether it is read).
    .PARAMETER Size
      The file size in bytes.
    .PARAMETER MaxFileMB
      The size limit.
    .OUTPUTS
      [bool] true when the file is to be read; false when it got its NOT SCANNED row.
    #>
    param($Rows, [hashtable]$Info, [string]$RawPath, [string]$Name, [long]$Size, [int]$MaxFileMB)
    foreach ($hit in @(Find-PiiInText $RawPath)) {
        $Rows.Add((New-ReportRow 'File name' $Info.Channel $Info.Item $Info.Link $Info.Modified $hit.Detector $hit.Confidence $hit.MaskedValue 'In the file or folder name'))
    }
    $reason = ''
    if (-not (Test-SupportedFile $Name)) { $reason = 'File type is not read by this script: check by hand' }
    elseif ($Size -gt ($MaxFileMB * 1MB)) { $reason = "Larger than $MaxFileMB MB: check by hand" }
    if ($reason) { $Rows.Add((New-ReportRow 'File' $Info.Channel $Info.Item $Info.Link $Info.Modified 'NOT SCANNED' '' '' $reason)) }
    return (-not $reason)
}

function Confirm-ScanStart {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Before a scan: how many files and MB were found, and how many will be listed but not read (type or size); then
      "Scan now? [Y/n]" (Enter = yes; the PAM, 2026-11-21: a guard against a scope larger than meant).
    .PARAMETER Groups
      The groups, each with Files.
    .PARAMETER Facts
      { param($File) } that gives @(name, size in bytes) of one file.
    .PARAMETER MaxFileMB
      The size limit.
    .PARAMETER NoAsk
      Show the counts but do not ask: the person already said yes to the whole list (several targets in one run).
    .OUTPUTS
      [bool] true to scan.
    #>
    param([object[]]$Groups, [scriptblock]$Facts, [int]$MaxFileMB, [switch]$NoAsk)
    $count = 0; $bytes = 0L; $skipped = 0
    foreach ($group in $Groups) {
        foreach ($file in $group.Files) {
            $name, $size = & $Facts $file
            $count++; $bytes += [long]$size
            if (-not (Test-SupportedFile $name) -or [long]$size -gt $MaxFileMB * 1MB) { $skipped++ }
        }
    }
    if ($count -eq 0) { return $true }   # nothing to read: no question
    # A count with its noun: "1 file", "2 files".
    $plural = { param($n, $noun) if ($n -eq 1) { "1 $noun" } else { '{0:N0} {1}s' -f $n, $noun } }
    Write-Ui ('Found {0} ({1:N1} MB) in {2}; {3:N0} will be listed but not read (file type, or over {4} MB).' -f
        (& $plural $count 'file'), ($bytes / 1MB), (& $plural $Groups.Count 'folder'), $skipped, $MaxFileMB) 'Text'
    if ($NoAsk) { return $true }
    $answer = Read-Host 'Scan now? [Y/n]'
    return -not ($answer -and $answer.Trim() -match '^(n|no)$')
}

function Invoke-ScanRun {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      One scan of the groups, for both scans: the "Scanning N files" banner, the meter, the readers, every group, then
      the rule and the total line. The run's meter, reading time and worker count go in Run, also when the scan
      stops part way (the caller's 'finally' reads them).
    .PARAMETER Init
      How the reader workers load their code (New-ReaderPool).
    .PARAMETER Rows
      The list that receives the rows.
    .PARAMETER Groups
      The groups, each with Name and Files.
    .PARAMETER StartFile
      { param($Queue, $Group, $File) } that starts one file (Invoke-GroupScan).
    .PARAMETER ShownFile
      { param($Group, $File) } that gives the file's masked path for the progress bar.
    .PARAMETER Run
      Receives Meter, Reading ([TimeSpan]) and Workers; Declined = $true when the person answered no to "Scan now?".
      AutoStart = $true (set by the caller): the counts show, but "Scan now?" is not asked.
    .PARAMETER Facts
      { param($File) } that gives @(name, size in bytes) of one file, for the question before the scan.
    .PARAMETER MaxFileMB
      The size limit (files over it are listed, not read).
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Init, $Rows, [object[]]$Groups, [scriptblock]$StartFile, [scriptblock]$ShownFile, [hashtable]$Run,
          [scriptblock]$Facts, [int]$MaxFileMB)
    $total = 0
    foreach ($group in $Groups) { $total += $group.Files.Count }
    $Run.Meter = New-ScanMeter 0; $Run.Reading = [TimeSpan]::Zero; $Run.Workers = 0; $Run.Declined = $false
    # Declined until a yes: a cancel (Ctrl+C) at the question saves nothing either, as nothing was read (review of PR 37).
    if ($Facts) { $Run.Declined = $true; if (-not (Confirm-ScanStart $Groups $Facts $MaxFileMB -NoAsk:($Run.ContainsKey('AutoStart') -and $Run.AutoStart))) { return }; $Run.Declined = $false }
    Write-Banner ('Scanning {0} files in {1} folders' -f $total, $Groups.Count)
    $meter = New-ScanMeter $total
    $meter.NameWidth = Get-NameWidth @($Groups | ForEach-Object { Protect-PiiName $_.Name })
    $Run.Meter = $meter; $Run.Reading = [TimeSpan]::Zero; $Run.Workers = 0
    $queue = New-ScanQueue $Init $Rows $meter
    try { Invoke-GroupScan $queue $Groups $StartFile $ShownFile; $Run.Workers = Get-QueueWorkers $queue }
    finally { $Run.Reading = Close-ScanQueue $queue }
    # The folder rows form one table; the total closes it, under a rule.
    Write-RuleLine
    Write-MeterLine $meter 'file' -Force
}

# ==== Module Accountability.psm1 ====
<#
Accountability.psm1 - who made and changed a file with PII, and who put the PII in and when.

  Purpose:     The PAM, 2026-11-21: for accountability and to date a possible incident, each file with a hit shows
               who last modified it, when it was made and by whom, and who added the PII and when, by e-mail
               address (the report is CUI and stays in DoD365). SharePoint and Teams: the file's fields and its
               version history (the first version that holds each hit). Drives: the file's owner (a Windows logon
               name, which Windows looks up from the owner's ID; no search of the directory: "No reconnaissance"), its times, and the names an Office file stores.
  Inputs:      the file's list item, path or reader result, and its hits.
  Outputs:     @{ ModifiedBy; Created; CreatedBy; AddedOn; AddedBy; HistoryNote; ByHit } for Report.psm1 Set-RowAccountability.
               "PII first saved" and "PII first saved by" hold only a date and a person (or "Not recorded"); every caveat goes in
               "History note" (the PAM, 2026-11-21: the columns must be easy to understand).
  How to run:  imported by Find-ChannelPII.ps1; not run on its own.
  Depends on:  TextExtract.psm1 and ReaderPool.psm1 (Invoke-LimitedRead), PiiDetectors.psm1 (Find-PiiInText) and, for SharePoint,
               PnP.PowerShell 1.12.0 (Get-PnPFileVersion, Get-PnPContext; read-only), imported first.
  Run target:  Windows PowerShell 5.1 (also PowerShell 7).
  Network:     only the signed-in site (version history of files with a hit).
  Writes:      none. Old versions are read in memory only.
  Credentials: none (the PnP sign-in of the scan).
#>


# Old versions read per file with a hit, oldest first (a file with more is dated from the oldest ones read).
$script:MaxVersionsRead = 30
$script:NotRecorded = 'Not recorded'
$script:NoHistory = 'The file has no version history'
# Put-backs named per hit in its history note (the rest are counted).
$script:MaxPutBacks = 3
$script:MaybeEarlier = 'An older version could not be read: it may have been added earlier'
$script:PartHistory = "Not in the old versions read: check the file's version history by hand"
$script:DriveNoHistory = 'A drive keeps no file history'
# The two fallbacks of "PII first saved by" say so in the History note (review of PR 41).
$script:NoOlderVersion = "No older version: the file's creator is named"
$script:OnlyCurrentVersion = 'In no older version: the current version is named'

function Get-PersonText {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      A person field as text: the e-mail address of a SharePoint user value, else its name, else the text itself.
    .PARAMETER Value
      A field value (FieldUserValue, User or text).
    .OUTPUTS
      [string]
    #>
    param($Value)
    if ($null -eq $Value) { return '' }
    foreach ($name in @('Email', 'LookupValue', 'Title')) {
        $property = $Value.PSObject.Properties[$name]
        if ($property -and $property.Value) { return [string]$property.Value }
    }
    return [string]$Value
}

function Get-DateText {
    # A date as yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm (the report's form); empty when missing.
    param($Value)
    if ($Value -is [datetime]) { return $Value.ToString('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') }
    if ($null -eq $Value) { return '' }
    return [string]$Value
}

function Get-SharePointWho {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Who made and last changed a SharePoint file, from its list item (Author, Created, Editor).
    .PARAMETER File
      The list item.
    .OUTPUTS
      [hashtable] ModifiedBy, Created, CreatedBy.
    #>
    param($File)
    return @{ ModifiedBy = (Get-PersonText $File['Editor']); Created = (Get-DateText $File['Created']); CreatedBy = (Get-PersonText $File['Author']) }
}

function Get-HitKey {
    # The same hit in two versions: its type and masked value.
    param($Hit)
    return ([string]$Hit.Detector + '|' + [string]$Hit.MaskedValue)
}

function Complete-CurrentVersion {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The current version's part: a hit missing from the newest old version (all of them read) was put back now; a
      hit in no old version came with the current one (with no old version: the file's maker) - only when every old
      version was read; else who added it is not known, and the report says so (review of PR 20).
    .PARAMETER ByHit
      Hit key -> @{ AddedOn; AddedBy; Note }.
    .PARAMETER Back
      Hit key -> put-back texts.
    .PARAMETER Present
      Hit key -> in the newest old version read.
    .PARAMETER Keys
      The hit keys.
    .PARAMETER Who
      Get-SharePointWho of the file, with Modified.
    .PARAMETER Read
      @{ AllRead; Complete; Versions }: how much of the history was read.
    #>
    param([hashtable]$ByHit, [hashtable]$Back, [hashtable]$Present, [object[]]$Keys, [hashtable]$Who, [hashtable]$Read)
    if ($Read.AllRead) {
        foreach ($key in @($Keys | Where-Object { $ByHit.ContainsKey($_) -and $Present.ContainsKey($_) -and $Present[$_] -eq $false })) {
            Add-PutBack $Back $key $Who.ModifiedBy $Who.Modified
        }
    }
    foreach ($key in @($Keys | Where-Object { -not $ByHit.ContainsKey($_) })) {
        if (-not $Read.Complete) { $ByHit[$key] = New-AddedEntry $script:NotRecorded $script:NotRecorded $script:PartHistory }
        elseif ($Read.Versions -eq 0) { $ByHit[$key] = New-AddedEntry $Who.Created $Who.CreatedBy $script:NoOlderVersion }
        else { $ByHit[$key] = New-AddedEntry $Who.Modified $Who.ModifiedBy $script:OnlyCurrentVersion }
    }
}

function Get-OldVersions {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      A file's old versions, oldest first (at most $MaxVersionsRead), with who saved each (read-only); $null when
      the history cannot be read.
    .PARAMETER Ref
      The file's server-relative path.
    .OUTPUTS
      [hashtable] Versions, All (how many there are), Context; or $null.
    #>
    param([string]$Ref)
    try {
        $all = @(Get-PnPFileVersion -Url $Ref -ErrorAction Stop | Sort-Object Created)
        $versions = @($all | Select-Object -First $script:MaxVersionsRead)
        $context = Get-PnPContext
        foreach ($version in $versions) { $context.Load($version.CreatedBy) }
        if ($versions.Count -gt 0) { $context.ExecuteQuery() }
        return @{ Versions = $versions; All = $all.Count; Context = $context }
    }
    catch { return $null }
}

function Read-VersionText {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The text of one old version of a file (read-only, in memory), or $null when it cannot be read or read in full.
    .PARAMETER Version
      A FileVersion of Get-PnPFileVersion.
    .PARAMETER Context
      The PnP client context.
    .PARAMETER Name
      The file name (for its reader).
    .OUTPUTS
      Output of Get-TextFromBytes, or $null.
    #>
    param($Version, $Context, [string]$Name)
    try {
        $stream = $Version.OpenBinaryStream(); $Context.ExecuteQuery()
        $memory = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream
        try { $stream.Value.CopyTo($memory); $bytes = $memory.ToArray() } finally { $memory.Dispose() }
        $text = Invoke-LimitedRead -Bytes $bytes -Name $Name   # with the per-file time limit, off the main thread (ReaderPool.psm1)
    }
    catch { return $null }
    if ($text.Status -ne 'OK') { return $null }
    return $text
}

function Add-PutBackText {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Adds the put-backs to the history note: "Put back by X on D" (at most $MaxPutBacks named, the rest counted),
      and notes what the history read cannot show.
    .PARAMETER ByHit
      Hit key -> @{ AddedOn; AddedBy; Note }.
    .PARAMETER Back
      Hit key -> the put-back texts (Add-PutBack).
    .PARAMETER Unsure
      Hit keys with a version not read after they were first seen: a removal and put-back there cannot be named.
    .PARAMETER AllRead
      Whether every old version was read (else later ones were not checked).
    #>
    param([hashtable]$ByHit, [hashtable]$Back, [hashtable]$Unsure, [bool]$AllRead)
    foreach ($key in $Back.Keys) {
        $list = $Back[$key]
        $text = @($list | Select-Object -First $script:MaxPutBacks) -join '; '
        $more = $list.Count - $script:MaxPutBacks
        if ($more -eq 1) { $text += '; and 1 more time' } elseif ($more -gt 1) { $text += ('; and {0} more times' -f $more) }
        Add-HistoryNote $ByHit[$key] $text
    }
    # Say what the history read cannot show, so "PII first saved by" never reads as complete when it is not (review).
    foreach ($key in @($ByHit.Keys | Where-Object { $ByHit[$_].AddedBy -ne $script:NotRecorded })) {
        if ($Unsure.ContainsKey($key)) { Add-HistoryNote $ByHit[$key] 'It may have been removed and put back (a version could not be read)' }
        if (-not $AllRead) { Add-HistoryNote $ByHit[$key] "Versions after the $script:MaxVersionsRead oldest were not checked" }
    }
}

function Add-PutBack {
    # Notes that a hit was put back (in a version after one without it): who saved that version and when.
    param([hashtable]$Back, [string]$Key, [string]$By, [string]$On)
    if (-not $Back.ContainsKey($Key)) { $Back[$Key] = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string] }
    $Back[$Key].Add(('Put back by {0} on {1}' -f $By, $On))
}

function New-AddedEntry {
    # Who added one hit and when, with an optional history note.
    param([string]$On, [string]$By, [string]$Note = '')
    return @{ AddedOn = $On; AddedBy = $By; Note = $Note }
}

function Add-HistoryNote {
    # Adds one sentence to a hit's history note ("; " between sentences).
    param([hashtable]$Entry, [string]$Text)
    if ($Entry.Note) { $Entry.Note += '; ' + $Text } else { $Entry.Note = $Text }
}

function Find-PiiAddedVersion {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      For each hit of a SharePoint file, the first version that holds it (who saved it and when), and each time it
      was put back after a version without it (the PAM, 2026-11-21). A hit
      in no older version came with the current one (the last modifier). Read-only; versions stay in memory.
    .PARAMETER Ref
      The file's server-relative path.
    .PARAMETER Name
      The file name (for its reader).
    .PARAMETER Hits
      The current hits (Detector, MaskedValue).
    .PARAMETER Who
      Get-SharePointWho of the file, with Modified (the current version's time).
    .OUTPUTS
      [hashtable] hit key -> @{ AddedOn; AddedBy; Note }.
    #>
    param([string]$Ref, [string]$Name, [object[]]$Hits, [hashtable]$Who)
    $byHit = @{}
    $open = @($Hits | ForEach-Object { Get-HitKey $_ } | Sort-Object -Unique)
    $history = Get-OldVersions $Ref
    if ($null -eq $history) {
        foreach ($key in $open) { $byHit[$key] = New-AddedEntry $script:NotRecorded $script:NotRecorded $script:NoHistory }
        return $byHit
    }
    $versions = $history.Versions; $context = $history.Context
    $complete = $history.All -le $script:MaxVersionsRead   # every older version is read (else a later one may add a hit)
    $allRead = $complete
    $readFailed = $false   # a version before this one could not be read (the cap only leaves newer ones unread)
    $keys = $open
    $present = @{}   # per hit: in the last version read ($true/$false), or not known after a version not read ($null)
    $back = @{}      # per hit: when it was put back after a version without it (the PAM, 2026-11-21)
    $unsure = @{}    # per hit: a version not read after the hit was seen: a removal and put-back there cannot be named
    foreach ($version in $versions) {   # every version read (not only to the first with each hit): put-backs count too
        $text = Read-VersionText $version $context $Name
        if ($null -eq $text) {   # not read: later versions can neither be first nor a put-back for sure
            $complete = $false; $readFailed = $true
            foreach ($key in $keys) {
                if ($byHit.ContainsKey($key)) { $unsure[$key] = $true }   # seen before: it may be removed and put back in this one
                $present[$key] = $null
            }
            continue
        }
        $found = @(Find-PiiInText $text.Text | ForEach-Object { Get-HitKey $_ })
        $on = Get-DateText $version.Created; $by = Get-PersonText $version.CreatedBy
        foreach ($key in $keys) {
            $has = $found -contains $key
            if ($has -and -not $byHit.ContainsKey($key)) {
                # After a version that could not be read, this may not be the first one with the hit (review of PR 20).
                if (-not $readFailed) { $byHit[$key] = New-AddedEntry $on $by }
                else { $byHit[$key] = New-AddedEntry $on $by $script:MaybeEarlier }
            }
            elseif ($has -and $present.ContainsKey($key) -and $present[$key] -eq $false) { Add-PutBack $back $key $by $on }
            $present[$key] = $has
        }
    }
    Complete-CurrentVersion $byHit $back $present $keys $Who @{ AllRead = $allRead; Complete = $complete; Versions = $versions.Count }
    Add-PutBackText $byHit $back $unsure $allRead
    return $byHit
}

function Get-DriveWho {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Who made and changed a file on a drive: its owner (usually its maker; a Windows logon name), its creation
      time, and the names an Office file stores (creator, last modified by). Windows does not record who last
      changed other files.
    .PARAMETER Path
      The file path.
    .PARAMETER Props
      The Office names of the reader result (Creator, LastModifiedBy), or $null.
    .OUTPUTS
      [hashtable] ModifiedBy, Created, CreatedBy, AddedOn, AddedBy, HistoryNote.
    #>
    param([string]$Path, [hashtable]$Props)
    $owner = ''
    try { $owner = (Get-Acl -LiteralPath $Path -ErrorAction Stop).Owner } catch { $owner = '' }   # no owner where the system keeps none
    $created = ''
    try { $created = (Get-Item -LiteralPath $Path -ErrorAction Stop).CreationTime.ToString('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') } catch { $created = '' }
    $modifiedBy = 'Not recorded (Windows keeps no last editor)'
    $createdBy = $owner
    if ($Props) {
        if ($Props.LastModifiedBy) { $modifiedBy = $Props.LastModifiedBy + ' (as saved in the file)' }
        if (-not $createdBy -and $Props.Creator) { $createdBy = $Props.Creator + ' (as saved in the file)' }
    }
    return @{ ModifiedBy = $modifiedBy; Created = $created; CreatedBy = $createdBy; AddedOn = $script:NotRecorded; AddedBy = $script:NotRecorded
        HistoryNote = $script:DriveNoHistory }
}

function Get-AccountRows {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The By account tab (C6, the PAM, 2026-11-21): one row per "PII first saved by" account, most hits first, with how
      many files and how many High, Medium and Low hits it first saved. Each file counts once per account; "Not
      recorded" and empty cells are left out. The same limits as the column apply (see the Method tab).
    .PARAMETER Hits
      The hit rows (High, Medium, Low).
    .OUTPUTS
      [object[]] rows: account, files, High, Medium, Low.
    #>
    param([object[]]$Hits)
    $byAccount = @{}
    foreach ($hit in $Hits) {
        $account = ([string]$hit.AddedBy).Trim()
        if (-not $account -or $account -eq $script:NotRecorded) { continue }
        if (-not $byAccount.ContainsKey($account)) { $byAccount[$account] = @{ Files = @{}; High = 0; Medium = 0; Low = 0 } }
        $entry = $byAccount[$account]
        $entry.Files[$hit.Channel + '/' + $hit.Item] = $true
        $entry[$hit.Confidence]++
    }
    $byTotal = @{ Expression = { $_.Value.High + $_.Value.Medium + $_.Value.Low }; Descending = $true }
    $byAccount.GetEnumerator() | Sort-Object $byTotal, Key | ForEach-Object {   # most hits first; equal totals by name (stable)
        , @($_.Key, [int]$_.Value.Files.Count, [int]$_.Value.High, [int]$_.Value.Medium, [int]$_.Value.Low)
    }
}

function Get-MethodRows {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The rows of the workbook's Method tab: how Ion finds possible PII, how sure each confidence is for each type (as
      src/PiiDetectors.psm1 decides it), and how it fills the who-and-when columns, with what that evidence can and
      cannot show (the PAM, 2026-11-21: the method on its own page; "PII first saved by" is evidence, not proof).
    .OUTPUTS
      [object[]] rows of two cells (topic, text).
    #>
    , @('How Ion finds possible PII', ('Ion reads the text of each file in memory and looks for patterns. A match is a lead, not proof: ' +
        'open each file and check it. The confidence (High, Medium, Low) depends on the type, as below.'))
    , @('SSN', ('Numbers the SSA never issues (000, 666 or 9xx first; 00 in the middle; 0000 last) are skipped. High: "SSN", "SSAN", ' +
        '"social security" or "soc sec" just before it. Medium: 3-2-4 digits with dashes or spaces. Low: 9 digits in a row. ' +
        'Without a label, 9 digits that go on into a longer number (a digit one space away, ' +
        'or a CAC name such as LAST.FIRST.M. just before) are not an SSN; nor are 9 digits inside an e-signature DoD ID (see DoD ID).'))
    , @('DoD ID', ('A 10-digit number, found only with a label just before it ("DoD ID", "DODID", "EDIPI", "CAC", "DoD #", "DoD number"), ' +
        'High. In an e-signature: Low, history not read ("Not looked up"); High with such a label. An e-signature is a CAC name then the ' +
        '10 digits, or the reverse, or the 10 digits after "signed", "signature", "CN=" or "DN:"; spaces, tabs and line breaks may ' +
        'split it. It is added only where no other match is. An SSN match (no label, no dashes) inside it becomes this DoD ID ' +
        'only after a signature word or when the digits touch the name.'))
    , @('Date of birth', ('A date written as 1/2/1990, 01-02-90, 1990-01-02, 2 Jan 1990, 02JAN1990 or January 2, 1990, found only with a label ' +
        'just before it ("DOB", "D.O.B.", "date of birth", "birth date", "birthdate", "born"): always High. Other date forms are not found.'))
    , @('Payment card', ('Medium: 13 to 19 digits (the length each card network uses), starting with that network''s digits, and the whole ' +
        'number passes the card check digit (Luhn). ' +
        'High: the same with a card word ("card", "Visa", "Amex", "GTC", "GPC" ...) before it.'))
    , @('Passport', 'Found only with "passport" just before it: always Medium.')
    , @('Phone', ('A US number written with separators: (###) ###-####, ###-###-####, ###.###.#### or ### ### ####; 10 digits in a row ' +
        'are not found; a +1 in front is allowed. Low: no label (office numbers are common). Medium: "cell", "mobile", "home", "personal", ' +
        '"my number" or "my phone" just before it.'))
    , @('Personal e-mail', 'Low: an address that does not end in .gov or .mil. Medium: a personal mail service (Gmail, Yahoo and the like).')
    , @('Home address', 'Low: number, street name and street type. Medium: it ends with a state and ZIP code. High: "home address" or similar before it.')
    , @('Name', 'Low: a name after "Name:", "Member:", "Spouse:" and the like. High: the same, with an SSN, DoD ID, date of birth, passport or card number close by.')
    , @('Noise', ('A Low match is dropped when, of the visible characters within 40 on each side (at least 20 of them), 30% or more are ' +
        'unreadable signs (symbols, a font read wrong). High and Medium matches are always kept.'))
    , @('')
    , @('Created, Created by', 'From SharePoint: when the file was first saved to the site, and by which account. On a drive: the file time and its owner.')
    , @('Last saved, Last saved by', 'The last save of the whole file, by anyone, for any reason. It says nothing about the PII; it shows who works on the file now.')
    , @('PII first saved, PII first saved by', (('Ion reads the file''s SharePoint version history, oldest first (at most {0} versions), and finds ' +
        'the first version whose text holds the same type and masked value. These columns name the account that saved that version, and when. ' +
        'If every older version was read and none holds the value, they name the current version (the same as Last saved). If the file has no ' +
        'older versions at all (version history off or never used), they name the file''s creator. If not every version could be read and none ' +
        'read holds the value, they say "Not recorded"; if a later version holds it, that version is named with a note that it may be earlier.') -f $script:MaxVersionsRead))
    , @('What that shows', ('SharePoint records which account saved each version. So these columns show which account first saved a version ' +
        'that holds the value: strong evidence, not proof of who typed it.'))
    , @('Why it can be wrong', ('The person who saves or uploads a file is not always the one who wrote the text: it may be pasted from an e-mail, ' +
        'copied from another file, or uploaded by OneDrive. When several people edit at once, SharePoint names one of them. Old versions may have ' +
        'been deleted. Two different values can look the same once masked (for example the same last 4 digits). A file copied in starts a new ' +
        'history. A drive keeps no history at all.'))
    , @('History note', ('Says how sure the two columns are: no version history; not in the versions read; a version that could not be read; ' +
        'a value removed and put back; more versions than were read; "No older version: the file''s creator is named"; "In no older ' +
        'version: the current version is named"; or, on a drive, "A drive keeps no file history".'))
    , @('Before you name a person', ('Open the file in SharePoint > Version history. Open the version Ion names and the one before it, and check ' +
        'that the full value first appears in that version.'))
    , @('By account tab', ('How many files and hits each "PII first saved by" account first saved, most first: a list of whom to contact first. ' +
        'It has the same limits as that column: evidence, not proof.'))
    , @('')
    , @('Review columns', ('Is it PII?, Action taken, Reported to Privacy Manager, Reviewed by and Review date (Hits tab) are for you to fill in as you ' +
        'check each hit. Ion leaves them empty. The first three have a dropdown list.'))
}

# ==== Module SiteTarget.psm1 ====
<#
SiteTarget.psm1 - what the person gives Ion to scan, and the sign-in to that one site.

  Purpose:     Reads a Teams link (a team's or a channel's), a SharePoint site address or a drive folder; turns a
               Teams link alone into its team's site (a lookup at the tenant root, about that one group); signs in
               to the site with the person's own CAC and checks that it belongs to the team in the link.
  Inputs:      the text the person pastes.
  Outputs:     a scan target (Get-ScanTarget) and the signed-in site address (Connect-TeamSite).
  Depends on:  PnP.PowerShell 1.12.0; PiiMasking.psm1 (Protect-PiiText), Report.psm1 (Write-Ui), imported first.
  How to run:  imported by Find-ChannelPII.ps1; not run on its own.
  Run target:  Windows PowerShell 5.1 (also PowerShell 7).
  Network:     the tenant root (one group-site lookup for a Teams link) and the one site; nothing lists other
               sites, teams or users ("No reconnaissance").
  Writes:      none.
  Credentials: the person's own CAC sign-in (PnP web login). Nothing is stored. One sign-in per host: each next site
               connects without a sign-out between sites; Find-ChannelPII.ps1 signs out once, at the end of the run
               (the PAM, 2026-11-24; probe 3, second run: docs/reviews/2026-11-24-device-probe-3.md).
#>


# What Ion can scan, for the first question and after a wrong paste (the PAM, 2026-11-21: one short block, not one long line).
$script:TargetHelp = "Paste one of these, or press Enter to exit:`n  - a Teams link: in Teams, ... next to the team or channel > Copy link`n" +
    "  - a SharePoint site address: https://usaf.dps.mil/teams/<team-site> (or /sites/)`n  - a drive folder: X:\<folder> or \\<server>\<share>\<folder>`n" +
    "  Several? Paste one, press Enter, paste the next; press Enter on an empty line to start (one workbook each)."

# A drive folder: X:\<folder> (a mapped drive) or \\<server>\<share>\<folder> (UNC), with or without quotes.
$script:DrivePathPattern = '^"?(?:[A-Za-z]:\\|\\\\[^\\/\s]+\\[^\\/]+)'

# Accepted site address: https://<host>/sites/<name> or /teams/<name>, also as a file's "Copy link"
# (https://<host>/:w:/r/teams/<name>/...). Anything after the site name is cut off, so the scan can only
# touch that one site. Other sharing links (/:w:/s/, /:w:/t/) are refused: they do not name the site.
$script:SiteUrlPattern = '^(?<host>https://[A-Za-z0-9.-]+)/(?::[a-z]:/r/)?(?<site>(?:sites|teams)/[^/?#\s]+)'

# The tenant root of DoD365: a Teams link alone is turned into its team's site here (GroupSiteManager GetSiteStatus, a
# read about the one group in the link; the PAM's test 2 of 2026-11-21 works). Only a site on this host is used.
# GetSiteStatus reports whether the group's site exists (0 not found, 1 being made, 2 ready); it is not the call that
# creates one (CreateGroupEx). Microsoft documents no write for it; not proven on DoD365 (review of PR 49).
$script:TenantRoot = 'https://usaf.dps.mil'

# When the SharePoint sign-in cannot work on this computer (for example Connect-PnPOnline blocked outside the US: the
# PAM, 2026-11-23), Ion does not stop: it shows how to scan a synced copy of the same files as a folder.
# Steps in ASD-STE100: one action each, with what the person sees next (review of PR 59).
$script:SyncSteps = "`nSharePoint sign-in did not work on this computer. You can scan a synced copy of the files as a folder." +
    "`nIf the library is open in your browser already, start at the step with Sync." +
    "`n  1. In Teams, click the channel. You see the channel's posts." +
    "`n  2. Click Files. You see the channel's files." +
    "`n  3. Click Open in SharePoint. You see the files in your browser." +
    "`n  4. Click Sync. You see a question to open OneDrive, or the sync starts." +
    "`n  5. If you see the question, click Open. Wait until File Explorer shows the synced folder." +
    "`n  6. In File Explorer, in the synced folder, click the address bar. You see the folder path selected." +
    "`n  7. Press Ctrl+C. The path is on the clipboard." +
    "`n  8. In Ion, at the question 'Scan more', press Ctrl+V. You see the path." +
    "`n  9. Press Enter. Ion scans the folder." +
    "`nFor your own OneDrive files, paste the path of your OneDrive folder at the question 'Scan more'."

# The longest one SharePoint call may wait for its answer (milliseconds), set on the signed-in connection when it has
# no limit or a longer one (the PnP 1.12.0 default is not verified here): a download, the file list, a version read.
# Without it a stalled call can wait without end, and Ctrl+C cannot stop a call in progress (the PAM's gov run,
# 2026-11-23: a scan hung after 50 minutes). 5 minutes: a 25 MB file on a slow link still downloads.
$script:SharePointCallMs = 300000

# Accepted Teams link: a team's or a channel's "Copy link" (https://<teams-host>/l/team/... or /l/channel/...).
# Only its groupId (the team's Microsoft 365 group) is used; the host is not, so any Teams cloud's link works.
# The path may hold spaces (a channel name) but not a second address ('://' or '/l/'), so text pasted after the link is not read.
$script:TeamsLinkPattern = '^https://[A-Za-z0-9.-]+/l/(?<kind>team|channel)/(?:(?!://|/l/)[^?#\r\n])+\?(?:[^#\s]*&)?groupId=' +
                           '(?<id>[0-9A-Fa-f]{8}-[0-9A-Fa-f]{4}-[0-9A-Fa-f]{4}-[0-9A-Fa-f]{4}-[0-9A-Fa-f]{12})(?![0-9A-Fa-f-])'

function Add-UrlScheme {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Adds https:// to an address typed without a scheme (usaf.dps.mil/teams/<team-site>); other text stays as it is.
    .PARAMETER Url
      The address the person gave.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string] the trimmed address, with https:// when it had no scheme.
    #>
    param([string]$Url)
    $Url = $Url.Trim()
    if ($Url -and $Url -notmatch '^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9+.-]*://') { return 'https://' + $Url }
    return $Url
}

function Test-TeamSiteUrl {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Checks that the address is one SharePoint site and returns it cut to the site name.
    .PARAMETER Url
      The address the person gave (it may go on to a library or a file, or be a file's "Copy link"; https:// may be left out).
    .OUTPUTS
      [string] https://<host>/sites/<name> or https://<host>/teams/<name>
    #>
    param([string]$Url)
    $match = [regex]::Match((Add-UrlScheme $Url), $script:SiteUrlPattern, 'IgnoreCase')
    if (-not $match.Success) {
        throw "'$Url' is not a SharePoint site address (https://usaf.dps.mil/teams/<team-site> or /sites/<team-site>)."
    }
    return $match.Groups['host'].Value + '/' + $match.Groups['site'].Value
}

function Get-ScanTarget {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Reads what the person gave: a Teams link (team or channel) or a team site address.
      Throws when it is neither.
    .PARAMETER Address
      The Teams link or the site address.
    .OUTPUTS
      [pscustomobject] GroupId ([guid] from a Teams link, else $null), SiteUrl (the site, else ''), IsChannel
      (true for a channel's link) and DrivePath (a drive or network folder, else '').
    #>
    param([string]$Address)
    # A folder on a mapped drive (X:\...) or a network share (\\server\share\...): scanned by DriveScan.psm1.
    if ($Address -match $script:DrivePathPattern) { return [pscustomobject]@{ GroupId = $null; SiteUrl = ''; IsChannel = $false; DrivePath = $Address.Trim('"') } }
    $link = [regex]::Match((Add-UrlScheme $Address), $script:TeamsLinkPattern, 'IgnoreCase')
    if ($link.Success) {
        return [pscustomobject]@{ GroupId = [guid]$link.Groups['id'].Value; SiteUrl = ''; IsChannel = ($link.Groups['kind'].Value -ieq 'channel'); DrivePath = '' }
    }
    if ($Address -match '/l/(team|channel)/') {
        throw 'This Teams link has no groupId. In Teams: the ... next to the team or channel name > Copy link.'
    }
    return [pscustomobject]@{ GroupId = $null; SiteUrl = (Test-TeamSiteUrl $Address); IsChannel = $false; DrivePath = '' }
}

function Assert-SiteGroup {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Checks that the connected site belongs to the team in the Teams link; throws when not, so nothing of another
      site is read (Connect-TeamSite returns no site; the run signs out at its end).
    .PARAMETER GroupId
      The group ID from the Teams link.
    #>
    param([guid]$GroupId)
    $actual = (Get-PnPSite -Includes GroupId -ErrorAction Stop).GroupId
    if ($actual -ne $GroupId) {
        throw 'This site does not belong to the team in the Teams link. Nothing was scanned.'
    }
}

function ConvertTo-FriendlyError {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      A sign-in error in words a person can act on: no access, site not found, sign-in not finished. Used only for
      the sign-in step (Connect-TeamSite); the original message always follows, masked, so no real error is hidden.
    .PARAMETER Message
      The error message.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string]
    #>
    param([string]$Message)
    $details = Protect-PiiText $Message
    if ($Message -match '\((401|403)\)|\bforbidden\b|access (is )?denied|do(es)? not have (access|permission)') {
        return "No access: your account cannot open this site. Ask the site's owner for access, or check the address. ($details)"
    }
    if ($Message -match '\(404\)|could not be resolved|remote name could not') { return "Site not found: check the address. ($details)" }
    if ($Message -match 'user cancel+ed|cancel+ed by the user|prompt cancel+ed') { return "The sign-in did not finish (was the sign-in window closed?). ($details)" }
    return $details
}

function Read-ScanTarget {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Asks for a Teams link, site address or drive folder until it is a valid one, or until the person presses Enter
      (exit); what can be pasted is listed first ($TargetHelp). A wrong paste is explained and asked again; nothing is
      signed in until the address is valid.
    .PARAMETER Given
      An address given up front (the -SiteUrl setting); used first, without asking.
    .PARAMETER Prompt
      The question to ask.
    .PARAMETER NoHelp
      Do not list what can be pasted first (after a scan; a wrong paste still lists it).
    .OUTPUTS
      Output of Get-ScanTarget, or $null when the person pressed Enter.
    #>
    param([string]$Given, [string]$Prompt, [switch]$NoHelp)
    $answer = $Given
    if (-not $answer -and -not $NoHelp) { Write-Ui $script:TargetHelp 'Muted' }
    while ($true) {
        if (-not $answer) { $answer = Read-Host $Prompt }
        if (-not $answer -or -not $answer.Trim()) { return $null }
        try { return Get-ScanTarget $answer.Trim() }
        catch {
            Write-Ui ('Not read: ' + (Protect-PiiText $_.Exception.Message)) 'Alert'
            Write-Ui $script:TargetHelp 'Muted'
            $answer = ''
        }
    }
}

function Read-TeamSiteAddress {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      For a Teams link: asks for the team's site address until it is a valid one; Enter gives up (an error the caller
      reports). Nothing is signed in here.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string] the site address, cut to the site.
    #>
    while ($true) {
        $typed = Read-Host 'Team site address (https://usaf.dps.mil/teams/<team-site>), or Enter to go back'
        if (-not $typed -or -not $typed.Trim()) { throw 'No site address given.' }
        try { return Test-TeamSiteUrl $typed }
        catch { Write-Ui (Protect-PiiText $_.Exception.Message) 'Alert' }
    }
}

function Find-TeamSiteByGroup {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The site of the one team in a Teams link: signs in at the tenant root (own CAC, web login) and asks SharePoint
      for that group's site (GroupSiteManager GetSiteStatus, a read; nothing is listed: "No reconnaissance"). Returns
      '' when the sign-in or the lookup fails, gives no site, or gives a site on another host (Data boundary): the
      caller then asks for the site address, as before.
    .PARAMETER GroupId
      The group ID from the Teams link.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string] https://<tenant root host>/sites/<name> or /teams/<name>, or ''.
    #>
    param([guid]$GroupId)
    Write-Ui "Teams link: looking up this team's site ..." 'Muted'
    Write-Ui '  If a sign-in window opens, sign in with your CAC. If you do not see the window, press Alt+Tab.' 'Muted'
    try {
        Connect-PnPOnline -Url $script:TenantRoot -UseWebLogin -WarningAction SilentlyContinue
        Set-SharePointTimeout
        $answer = Invoke-PnPSPRestMethod -Method Post -Url '/_api/GroupSiteManager/GetSiteStatus' -Content @{ groupId = [string]$GroupId } -ErrorAction Stop
    }
    catch { Write-Verbose ('Team site lookup not possible: ' + $_.Exception.Message); return '' }
    $url = ''
    if ($answer -and $answer.PSObject.Properties['SiteUrl']) { $url = [string]$answer.SiteUrl }
    if (-not $url) { return '' }
    try { $site = Test-TeamSiteUrl $url } catch { return '' }   # not a site address: ask instead
    if (-not $site.StartsWith($script:TenantRoot + '/', [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { return '' }
    return $site
}

function Set-SharePointTimeout {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Sets the time limit of each SharePoint call on the signed-in connection ($SharePointCallMs). A call over it fails
      with a time-out: a download then gets a NOT SCANNED row, and the scan goes on.
    #>
    try { $context = Get-PnPContext } catch { Write-Verbose 'No client context: no time limit set.'; return }   # never a sign-in error
    if (-not ($context -and $context.PSObject.Properties['RequestTimeout'])) { return }
    if ($context.RequestTimeout -le 0 -or $context.RequestTimeout -gt $script:SharePointCallMs) { $context.RequestTimeout = $script:SharePointCallMs }
}

function Assert-ConnectedSite {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Checks that the signed-in connection reaches the given site: its web's full address (host and path) equals the
      site's address. A sign-in without a sign-out keeps the last site's connection when it fails; this check makes
      sure that no read goes to that other site (also on another host). Throws when the web is another site, or cannot
      be read (no access).
    .PARAMETER Site
      The site address that was signed in to.
    #>
    param([string]$Site)
    $want = [uri]::UnescapeDataString(([uri]$Site).GetLeftPart([UriPartial]::Path)).TrimEnd('/')
    $web = Get-PnPWeb -Includes Url -ErrorAction Stop
    $actual = [uri]::UnescapeDataString([string]$web.Url).TrimEnd('/')
    if (-not [string]::Equals($actual, $want, [StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) {
        throw 'The sign-in did not reach this site. Nothing was scanned. Close Windows PowerShell ISE. Open it again. Then run Ion again.'
    }
}

function Connect-TeamSite {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Signs in (own CAC, PnP web login) to the site and returns its address. For a Teams link (a team's or a
      channel's) it looks the site up at the tenant root (Find-TeamSiteByGroup), and asks for the site address only
      when that fails; it then checks that the site belongs to the team in the link. It then checks that the connection
      reaches that site (Assert-ConnectedSite) and that the person can open it. On any error it throws a message a
      person can act on (ConvertTo-FriendlyError). It does not sign out: the next site connects without a sign-out,
      and Find-ChannelPII.ps1 signs out once at the end of the run, after an error or Ctrl+C too (the PAM, 2026-11-24).
    .PARAMETER Target
      Output of Get-ScanTarget.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string] the site the scan may touch.
    #>
    param($Target)
    $site = $Target.SiteUrl
    $signingIn = $false   # true only while the sign-in itself runs: only its errors get the sync steps (review of PR 59)
    try {
        if ($null -ne $Target.GroupId) {
            if ($Target.IsChannel) {
                Write-Ui 'Channel link: the scan covers the standard channels of its team. A private or shared channel has its own site: give that site address instead.' 'Accent'
            }
            $site = Find-TeamSiteByGroup $Target.GroupId
            if (-not $site) {
                Write-Ui "The team's site could not be looked up: the script needs its address. In Teams: the team's Shared tab > ... > Open in SharePoint, then copy the address." 'Accent'
                $site = Read-TeamSiteAddress
            }
        }
        Write-Ui ('Signing in to ' + (Protect-PiiName $site) + ' ...') 'Muted'
        Write-Ui '  If a sign-in window opens, sign in with your CAC. If you do not see the window, press Alt+Tab. If it says that you have no access, close it.' 'Muted'
        # PnP prints a warning that suggests -Interactive (and its update notice): not useful to the person, so hidden (the PAM, 2026-11-20).
        $signingIn = $true
        Connect-PnPOnline -Url $site -UseWebLogin -WarningAction SilentlyContinue
        $signingIn = $false
        Set-SharePointTimeout
        Assert-ConnectedSite $site   # also: can this person open the site? A refusal here is "no access", before any scan
        if ($null -ne $Target.GroupId) { Assert-SiteGroup $Target.GroupId }
        return $site
    }
    catch {
        $friendly = ConvertTo-FriendlyError $_.Exception.Message
        # Not "no access", "not found" or "sign-in not finished": the sign-in itself does not work here (blocked, missing).
        if ($signingIn -and $friendly -eq (Protect-PiiText $_.Exception.Message)) { $friendly += $script:SyncSteps }
        throw $friendly
    }
}

function Read-ScanTargetList {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Asks for what to scan, then "Add another, or press Enter to start" until an empty line: one or more targets
      (the PAM, 2026-11-24: several sites per run, one workbook each). Each target is checked as it is pasted
      (Read-ScanTarget); only the targets the person pastes are touched ("No reconnaissance").
    .PARAMETER Given
      A target given up front (the -SiteUrl setting): the list is that one target, without asking.
    .PARAMETER Prompt
      The first question.
    .PARAMETER NoHelp
      Do not list what can be pasted first.
    .OUTPUTS
      [object[]] outputs of Get-ScanTarget; empty when the person pressed Enter at the first question.
    #>
    param([string]$Given, [string]$Prompt, [switch]$NoHelp)
    $first = Read-ScanTarget $Given $Prompt -NoHelp:$NoHelp
    if ($null -eq $first) { return @() }
    $list = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object]
    $list.Add($first)
    if ($Given) { return $list.ToArray() }
    while ($true) {
        $next = Read-ScanTarget '' ('Add another ({0} so far), or press Enter to start' -f $list.Count) -NoHelp
        if ($null -eq $next) { break }
        $list.Add($next)
    }
    return $list.ToArray()
}

function Get-TargetLabel {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      A target in a few words for the list and the closing table: the site address or the drive folder (both masked
      like any name Ion shows) or "Teams link (team)" / "Teams link (channel)".
    .PARAMETER Target
      Output of Get-ScanTarget.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string]
    #>
    param($Target)
    if ($Target.DrivePath) { return Protect-PiiName $Target.DrivePath }
    if ($Target.SiteUrl) { return Protect-PiiName $Target.SiteUrl }
    if ($Target.IsChannel) { return 'Teams link (channel)' }
    return 'Teams link (team)'
}

# ==== Module ChannelScan.psm1 ====
<#
ChannelScan.psm1 - reads one team's or site's files and turns them into report rows.

  Purpose:     The SharePoint and Teams steps of Find-ChannelPII.ps1 after the sign-in (SiteTarget.psm1), one
               function per step: find the library, list the channel folders, scan each file with a progress bar
               and live counts. Posts are not read (the PAM,
               2026-11-20: one sign-in only; reading posts needs an admin-approved Graph permission).
  Inputs:      the signed-in site (SiteTarget.psm1 Connect-TeamSite).
  Outputs:     report rows (Report.psm1 New-ReportRow) added to the list the caller passes in.
  Depends on:  PnP.PowerShell 1.12.0; PiiDetectors.psm1, PiiMasking.psm1, TextExtract.psm1, Report.psm1,
               ScanMeter.psm1, SiteTarget.psm1 (all imported first by Find-ChannelPII.ps1).
  How to run:  imported by Find-ChannelPII.ps1; not run on its own.
  Run target:  Windows PowerShell 5.1 (also PowerShell 7).
  Network:     only the one signed-in SharePoint site (SiteTarget.psm1 signs in). Nothing lists other sites or teams (no network
               reconnaissance).
  Writes:      none in SharePoint or Teams: read calls only. Rows go to an in-memory list.
  Credentials: the person's own CAC sign-in (PnP web login). Nothing is stored.
#>


# =====================================================================================
# Settings
# =====================================================================================

# A team site's file library: Teams names it "Documents" (URL "Shared Documents"). It is scanned first.
$script:LibraryNames = @('Shared Documents', 'Documents')

# A document library is list template 101 (SharePoint ListTemplateType.DocumentLibrary).
$script:DocumentLibraryTemplate = 101

# Visible libraries that hold SharePoint's own files, not people's files: not scanned (the PAM, 2026-11-20: Site Assets,
# Preservation Hold Library and Site Collection Documents are scanned).
$script:NotScannedLibraries = @('Style Library', 'Form Templates')

# Teams makes one top-level folder per standard channel. "Forms" is SharePoint's own folder, not a channel.
$script:NotChannelFolders = @('Forms')

# Time spent downloading and spent reading, for the summary (it shows where a slow scan loses its time).
# The per-file bar's heading while a file comes down from SharePoint into memory (never to disk). ISE adds a "." after a
# bar's heading, so two here show as "..." (the PAM's layout test E, 2026-11-21).
$script:DownloadStep = 'Downloading..'

$script:Timing = @{ Download = [TimeSpan]::Zero; Check = [TimeSpan]::Zero }

# Streamed download (Get-FileBytes, the PAM 2026-11-21): File.OpenBinaryDirect on the signed-in connection, so the bar
# counts bytes; $false for the rest of a scan after one failure. Tests replace the opener.
$script:DirectDownload = $true
# Opens the file as a stream on the signed-in PnP connection.
$script:OpenDirectStream = {
    param([string]$ServerRelativeUrl)
    if (-not ($fileType = 'Microsoft.SharePoint.Client.File' -as [type])) { throw 'The SharePoint client library is not loaded.' }
    return $fileType::OpenBinaryDirect((Get-PnPContext), $ServerRelativeUrl).Stream
}

# How the report and the screen name the files at the top of the library (in no folder).
$script:RootGroupName = '(top of the library)'

# Office types that SharePoint opens by file ID (Doc.aspx?sourcedoc=): the link for a file whose path holds PII.
$script:IdLinkExtensions = @('.docx', '.docm', '.doc', '.xlsx', '.xlsm', '.xls', '.pptx', '.pptm', '.ppt')

# Items per request when listing the library. SharePoint refuses views over 5,000 items
# (list view threshold); 500 keeps each request small.
$script:ListPageSize = 500

# =====================================================================================
# Site, library and channels
# =====================================================================================

function Get-SiteDocumentLibraries {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The document libraries of the connected site that hold people's files: every visible library (template 101),
      without SharePoint's own ($NotScannedLibraries). The team's "Documents" library comes first. Only this one
      site: subsites are not read (the PAM, 2026-11-20).
    .OUTPUTS
      [pscustomobject] Title, List (the PnP list), ServerRelativeUrl (/sites/<name>/Shared Documents),
      SiteRelativeUrl (Shared Documents); one per library.
    #>
    $lists = @(Get-PnPList -ErrorAction Stop | Where-Object {
        [int]$_.BaseTemplate -eq $script:DocumentLibraryTemplate -and -not $_.Hidden -and $script:NotScannedLibraries -notcontains $_.Title
    })
    $libraries = foreach ($list in $lists) {
        $rootUrl = (Get-PnPProperty -ClientObject $list -Property RootFolder -ErrorAction Stop).ServerRelativeUrl.TrimEnd('/')
        $siteRelative = $rootUrl -replace '^.*?/(sites|teams)/[^/]+/', ''
        [pscustomobject]@{ Title = [string]$list.Title; List = $list; ServerRelativeUrl = $rootUrl; SiteRelativeUrl = $siteRelative }
    }
    $libraries = @($libraries | Sort-Object @{ Expression = { $script:LibraryNames -notcontains $_.SiteRelativeUrl } }, Title)
    if ($libraries.Count -eq 0) { throw 'No document library with files on this site. Check the address, or ask the site owner.' }
    return $libraries
}

function Get-ChannelFolderNames {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Lists the channel folders: the top-level folders of the library, without SharePoint's own. The scan
      covers all of them (the PAM, 2026-11-20: no channel choice). A plain SharePoint site may have none.
    .PARAMETER Library
      One library of Get-SiteDocumentLibraries.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string[]] folder names, sorted.
    #>
    param($Library)
    $folders = Get-PnPFolderItem -FolderSiteRelativeUrl $Library.SiteRelativeUrl -ItemType Folder -ErrorAction Stop
    return @($folders | ForEach-Object { $_.Name } | Where-Object { $script:NotChannelFolders -notcontains $_ } | Sort-Object)
}

# =====================================================================================
# Files
# =====================================================================================

function Get-FieldValue {
    # Reads one field of a list item as text (dates as yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm); empty when missing.
    param($Item, [string]$Name)
    $value = $Item[$Name]
    if ($null -eq $value) { return '' }
    if ($value -is [datetime]) { return $value.ToString('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm') }
    return [string]$value
}

function Get-SafeLink {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      A link for the report that holds no PII and opens the exact file (the PAM, 2026-11-20). It is the file's own
      path when its path below the library holds no PII (Protect-PiiName on the whole path changes nothing). Else, for an Office file,
      a link by the file's ID (<site>/_layouts/15/Doc.aspx?sourcedoc={id}), which names nothing; else the deepest
      folder whose name is clean (at least the library). Each part is escaped, so names with '#', '%' or spaces work.
    .PARAMETER Origin
      https://<host>
    .PARAMETER ServerRelativeUrl
      /sites/<name>/<library>/<folder>/<file>
    .PARAMETER UniqueId
      The file's ID (the list field UniqueId), or empty.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string]
    #>
    param([string]$Origin, [string]$ServerRelativeUrl, [string]$UniqueId = '')
    $parts = $ServerRelativeUrl.TrimStart('/').Split('/')
    # Joins path parts into a link path, each part escaped.
    $escape = { param($names) ($names | ForEach-Object { [uri]::EscapeDataString($_) }) -join '/' }
    # The first three parts are the site and the library, which the person gave or Teams named.
    $keep = [Math]::Min(3, $parts.Count)
    # Each step checks the whole path below the library, so a label in a parent folder ("CAC/<number>") is seen.
    while ($keep -lt $parts.Count) {
        $below = $parts[3..$keep] -join '/'
        if ((Protect-PiiName $below) -cne $below) { break }
        $keep++
    }
    if ($keep -eq $parts.Count) { return $Origin + '/' + (& $escape $parts) }
    $id = [guid]::Empty
    $extension = [System.IO.Path]::GetExtension($parts[-1]).ToLower()
    if ($parts.Count -ge 2 -and $script:IdLinkExtensions -contains $extension -and [guid]::TryParse($UniqueId, [ref]$id)) {
        return '{0}/{1}/_layouts/15/Doc.aspx?sourcedoc=%7B{2}%7D' -f $Origin, (& $escape $parts[0..1]), $id
    }
    return $Origin + '/' + (& $escape $parts[0..($keep - 1)])
}

function Get-ShownItem {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      A file's path inside its folder, as the report and the progress bar show it: PII masked, checked together with
      the folder name, so a label in the folder ("CAC", "DOB") and a number in the file name are seen as one hit.
    .PARAMETER Channel
      The folder (channel) name, not masked.
    .PARAMETER RawItem
      The path inside the folder, not masked.
    .OUTPUTS
      [string] the masked path inside the folder.
    #>
    param([string]$Channel, [string]$RawItem)
    $masked = Protect-PiiName ($Channel + '/' + $RawItem)
    $cut = $masked.IndexOf('/')
    # A hit across the folder's slash: fall back to the strict mask of the path alone.
    if ($cut -lt 0) { return Protect-PiiText $RawItem }
    return $masked.Substring($cut + 1)
}

function Get-FileBytes {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Downloads one file into memory (never to disk).
    .DESCRIPTION
      A file large enough for the per-file bar is read from SharePoint as a stream (a read: HTTP GET of the file on the
      signed-in connection, same site, same rights), a chunk at a time, so the bar shows the MB done. If that fails,
      or for a small file, Get-PnPFile -AsMemoryStream gives the whole file in one call; if this PnP version has no
      such switch, the SharePoint client call OpenBinaryStream on the same connection does.
    .PARAMETER ServerRelativeUrl
      /sites/<name>/<library>/<folder>/<file>
    .PARAMETER Size
      The file size from the list (0 when not known).
    .OUTPUTS
      [byte[]]
    #>
    param([string]$ServerRelativeUrl, [long]$Size = 0)
    # '#' and '%' in a path break the stream call's address: such a file takes the normal download.
    if ($script:DirectDownload -and (Test-FileBarSize $Size) -and $ServerRelativeUrl.IndexOfAny([char[]]'#%') -lt 0) {
        try {
            $stream = & $script:OpenDirectStream $ServerRelativeUrl
            try { $bytes = Copy-StreamWithProgress $stream $Size $script:DownloadStep } finally { $stream.Dispose() }
            if ($bytes.LongLength -eq $Size) { return , $bytes }
            # Not the size the list gives (a sign-in page, for example): the normal download decides.
            throw ('the stream gave {0} bytes, the list says {1}' -f $bytes.LongLength, $Size)
        }
        catch {
            # The streamed download does not work with this sign-in or PnP version: the normal one for the rest of the scan.
            $script:DirectDownload = $false
            Write-Verbose ('Streamed download not possible, normal download used: ' + $_.Exception.Message)
        }
    }
    Write-FileStep $Size $script:DownloadStep   # no byte count: the bar says so
    # The leading commas keep the byte array whole: PowerShell would otherwise unroll it, byte by byte (slow).
    try {
        $stream = Get-PnPFile -Url $ServerRelativeUrl -AsMemoryStream -ErrorAction Stop
        return , $stream.ToArray()
    }
    catch [System.Management.Automation.ParameterBindingException] {
        # This PnP version has no -AsMemoryStream: use the client call instead (same connection, same rights).
        $context = Get-PnPContext
        $binary = $context.Web.GetFileByServerRelativeUrl($ServerRelativeUrl).OpenBinaryStream()
        $context.ExecuteQuery()
        $memory = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream
        try { $binary.Value.CopyTo($memory); return , $memory.ToArray() } finally { $memory.Dispose() }
    }
}

function Get-FilesByFolder {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The files (not folders) of a library, sorted once by their top-level folder (any depth below it); the files at
      the top of the library, in no folder, under the key ''. One pass over the items, not one per folder (speed).
    .PARAMETER AllItems
      Every item of the library (one Get-PnPListItem call for all channels).
    .PARAMETER LibraryUrl
      /sites/<name>/<library>
    .OUTPUTS
      [hashtable] top-level folder name (any letter case) -> list of items.
    #>
    param([object[]]$AllItems, [string]$LibraryUrl)
    $prefix = $LibraryUrl + '/'
    $byFolder = @{}
    foreach ($item in $AllItems) {
        if ((Get-FieldValue $item 'FSObjType') -ne '0') { continue }
        $ref = Get-FieldValue $item 'FileRef'
        if (-not $ref.StartsWith($prefix, [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase)) { continue }
        $slash = $ref.IndexOf('/', $prefix.Length)
        $folder = ''; if ($slash -ge 0) { $folder = $ref.Substring($prefix.Length, $slash - $prefix.Length) }
        if (-not $byFolder.ContainsKey($folder)) { $byFolder[$folder] = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[object] }
        $byFolder[$folder].Add($item)
    }
    return $byFolder
}

function Invoke-OneFileScan {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Starts one file: a hit for PII in its name, then a NOT SCANNED row (unsupported, too large, download failed)
      or its download, handed to the readers (its text rows follow when a worker finishes).
    .PARAMETER Queue
      Output of New-ScanQueue.
    .PARAMETER File
      The list item.
    .PARAMETER Channel
      The channel folder name.
    .PARAMETER Prefix
      The channel folder's server-relative path with a trailing '/'.
    .PARAMETER Origin
      https://<host>
    .PARAMETER MaxFileMB
      The size limit.
    .OUTPUTS
      [bool] true when the file went to the readers (else its rows are in and it still needs counting).
    #>
    param([hashtable]$Queue, $File, [string]$Channel, [string]$Prefix, [string]$Origin, [int]$MaxFileMB)
    $rows = $Queue.Rows
    $name = Get-FieldValue $File 'FileLeafRef'
    $ref = Get-FieldValue $File 'FileRef'
    $rawChannel = $Channel
    $Channel = Protect-PiiName $Channel   # from here on, the channel name as the report shows it
    $modified = Get-FieldValue $File 'Modified'
    # The path is shown in the report: mask PII in it (with its folder name), and report a name that holds PII as a hit.
    $rawItem = $ref.Substring($Prefix.Length)
    $item = Get-ShownItem $rawChannel $rawItem
    $link = Get-SafeLink $Origin $ref (Get-FieldValue $File 'UniqueId')
    $size = 0L
    [void][long]::TryParse((Get-FieldValue $File 'File_x0020_Size'), [ref]$size)
    $info = @{ Channel = $Channel; Item = $item; Link = $link; Modified = $modified }
    if (-not (Add-FileStartRows $rows $info ($rawChannel + '/' + $rawItem) $name $size $MaxFileMB)) { return $false }
    $clock = [Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()
    try { $bytes = Get-FileBytes $ref $size }
    catch {
        # One file that cannot be downloaded must not stop the scan: note it and go on.
        $reason = Protect-PiiText ('Download failed: ' + $_.Exception.Message)
        $rows.Add((New-ReportRow 'File' $Channel $item $link $modified 'NOT SCANNED' '' '' $reason)); return $false
    }
    finally { $script:Timing.Download += $clock.Elapsed; Write-Progress -Id 2 -Activity $script:DownloadStep -Completed }
    # The main thread downloads the next file while workers read this one (speed, the PAM 2026-11-21).
    # Accountability: who made and changed the file; for a file with a hit, who added each hit (version history).
    $who = Get-SharePointWho $File
    $who.Modified = $modified
    $info.Who = $who
    $info.Added = { param($hits) Find-PiiAddedVersion $ref $name $hits $who }.GetNewClosure()
    Submit-ScanFile $Queue $info $name $bytes ''
    return $true
}

function Get-ScanTiming {
    # The time of the last file scan spent downloading and spent reading, as minutes:seconds text.
    [ordered]@{ 'Time downloading' = (Format-Duration $script:Timing.Download); 'Time reading files (all readers)' = (Format-Duration $script:Timing.Check) }
}

function Get-ScanGroups {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      The scan groups of one library: one per top-level folder, then the files at the top of the library.
    .PARAMETER Library
      One library of Get-SiteDocumentLibraries.
    .PARAMETER Folders
      The top-level folder names.
    .PARAMETER Label
      Put before each group name ("Library / ") when the site has several libraries; else empty.
    .OUTPUTS
      [pscustomobject] Name, Prefix, Files; a group without files is kept only when it is a folder.
    #>
    param($Library, [string[]]$Folders, [string]$Label)
    $fields = @('FileLeafRef', 'FileRef', 'FSObjType', 'File_x0020_Size', 'Modified', 'UniqueId', 'Created', 'Author', 'Editor')
    $allItems = @(Get-PnPListItem -List $Library.List -PageSize $script:ListPageSize -Fields $fields -ErrorAction Stop)
    $byFolder = Get-FilesByFolder $allItems $Library.ServerRelativeUrl
    foreach ($folder in $Folders) {
        $files = @(); if ($byFolder.ContainsKey($folder)) { $files = $byFolder[$folder].ToArray() }
        [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $Label + $folder; Prefix = ($Library.ServerRelativeUrl + '/' + $folder + '/'); Files = $files }
    }
    $rootFiles = @(); if ($byFolder.ContainsKey('')) { $rootFiles = $byFolder[''].ToArray() }
    if ($rootFiles.Count -gt 0) {
        [pscustomobject]@{ Name = $Label + $script:RootGroupName; Prefix = $Library.ServerRelativeUrl + '/'; Files = $rootFiles }
    }
}

function Invoke-FileScan {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      Scans every file in the given libraries' top-level folders and at their top, and adds report rows.
    .PARAMETER Library
      One or more libraries of Get-SiteDocumentLibraries.
    .PARAMETER Channels
      The channel folder names to scan when there is one library; with several libraries, each library's own
      top-level folders are scanned.
    .PARAMETER SiteUrl
      The site address (for the links in the report).
    .PARAMETER MaxFileMB
      Files larger than this are reported as NOT SCANNED.
    .PARAMETER Rows
      The list that receives the rows.
    .PARAMETER Init
      How the reader workers load their code (New-ReaderPool).
    .PARAMETER Progress
      Optional; gets Meter, Reading, Workers and Declined (Invoke-ScanRun), so a scan stopped part way can still say how
      many files were read, and a "no" to "Scan now?" saves nothing.
    .OUTPUTS
      [int] the number of files whose text was scanned.
    #>
    param($Library, [string[]]$Channels, [string]$SiteUrl, [int]$MaxFileMB, $Rows, [hashtable]$Init, [hashtable]$Progress)
    $origin = ([uri]$SiteUrl).GetLeftPart([System.UriPartial]::Authority)
    $script:Timing = @{ Download = [TimeSpan]::Zero; Check = [TimeSpan]::Zero }
    $script:DirectDownload = $true   # tried again on each site
    $libraries = @($Library)
    $groups = @(foreach ($one in $libraries) {
        if ($libraries.Count -eq 1) { Get-ScanGroups $one $Channels ''; continue }
        # Several libraries: one you cannot open (Preservation Hold Library often) is one BLOCKED row; the rest go on.
        # Collected first, so a library that fails part way adds no groups next to its BLOCKED row.
        try { $libraryGroups = @(Get-ScanGroups $one @(Get-ChannelFolderNames $one) ($one.Title + ' / ')); $libraryGroups }   # "Library / Folder"
        catch {
            $Rows.Add((New-ReportRow 'Scan' (Protect-PiiName $one.Title) '(library)' '' '' 'BLOCKED' '' '' ('Could not read this library: ' + (Protect-PiiText $_.Exception.Message))))
        }
    })
    # Starts one file of a group (Invoke-GroupScan calls it).
    $start = { param($queue, $group, $file) Invoke-OneFileScan $queue $file $group.Name $group.Prefix $origin $MaxFileMB }
    # The file's masked path inside its folder, for the progress bar.
    $shown = { param($group, $file) Get-ShownItem $group.Name ((Get-FieldValue $file 'FileRef').Substring($group.Prefix.Length)) }
    $run = $Progress; if (-not $run) { $run = @{} }
    # A file's name and size, for the question before the scan.
    $facts = { param($file) $size = 0L; [void][long]::TryParse((Get-FieldValue $file 'File_x0020_Size'), [ref]$size); (Get-FieldValue $file 'FileLeafRef'), $size }
    try { Invoke-ScanRun $Init $Rows $groups $start $shown $run $facts $MaxFileMB }
    finally { if ($run.ContainsKey('Reading')) { $script:Timing.Check = $run.Reading } }
    return $run.Meter.Read
}

# =====================================================================================
# Team or plain site
# =====================================================================================

function Test-TeamSite {
    <#
    .SYNOPSIS
      True when the connected site belongs to a team (a Microsoft 365 group): its folders are then channels.
      The report names the folder column "Channel" for a team and "Folder" for a plain site (the PAM, 2026-11-20).
    .OUTPUTS
      [bool]
    #>
    $groupId = (Get-PnPSite -Includes GroupId -ErrorAction Stop).GroupId
    return [bool]($groupId -and $groupId -ne [guid]::Empty)
}

# ==== Module DriveScan.psm1 ====
<#
DriveScan.psm1 - scans a folder on a mapped or network drive (X:\<folder> or \\<server>\<share>\<folder>).

  Purpose:     The PAM, 2026-11-21: the same check as for a SharePoint site, for one folder and all its subfolders on
               a drive the person can open. Nothing above the folder, no other share or server is touched ("No
               reconnaissance"). Files are opened read-only and shared, with the person's own Windows logon.
  Inputs:      the pasted folder path; the reader workers' set-up (New-ReaderPool).
  Outputs:     report rows; the results workbook (Report.psm1 Publish-ScanReport).
  How to run:  imported by Find-ChannelPII.ps1; Invoke-DriveScan runs when the pasted target is a folder path.
  Depends on:  PiiDetectors.psm1, PiiMasking.psm1, TextExtract.psm1 (Test-SupportedFile), Report.psm1,
               ScanMeter.psm1, ScanQueue.psm1 and ChannelScan.psm1 (Get-ShownItem), imported first.
  Run target:  Windows PowerShell 5.1 (also PowerShell 7, where the tests run on a temporary folder).
  Network:     only the given folder's server, through the person's Windows logon (SMB); in a OneDrive folder, OneDrive
               downloads an online-only file only after the person answers y (Select-OnlineFiles).
  Writes:      only the results workbook under the results folder (a downloaded OneDrive file is written by OneDrive).
  Credentials: none (the Windows logon).
#>


# Files directly in the pasted folder form this group (each subfolder is its own group).
$script:DriveTopGroup = '(top of the folder)'
# A file link in the folder is never read (the PAM, 2026-11-21: "No reconnaissance").
$script:FileLinkReason = 'A link to another file is not followed (it can lead outside the folder): check by hand'
# OneDrive (and a SharePoint library synced by OneDrive) keeps its files and folders as reparse points too: they are the
# item itself, not a link, so they are read (the PAM, 2026-11-23: scan a synced OneDrive folder when SharePoint sign-in
# is blocked). A file whose data is only in the cloud has one of these attributes: RecallOnDataAccess (0x400000),
# RecallOnOpen (0x40000) or Offline (0x1000). Ion asks before it reads such files (the PAM, 2026-11-23: "Ask each run").
$script:OnlineOnlyAttributes = 0x441000
$script:OnlineReason = 'Online only (OneDrive), not downloaded: check by hand, or run Ion again and answer y to download'

function Get-OneDriveHomes {
    # The person's own OneDrive folders ("My files"), from Windows' OneDrive environment variables: only these can hold
    # shortcuts to other teams' libraries (Confirm-OneDriveRoot). A synced library folder is not one (review of PR 59).
